# Experiment log

One row per tested variant. Source of every deleted bot: `git checkout archive/pre-cleanup -- bots/NAME`. Variants not listed here have no recorded result; their source is in the same tag.

**Columns:** Local = wins in the standard local run (560 games: 40 maps x 7 pool bots x 2 sides) unless noted. Platform = wins/games on a panel (see `docs/lineage.md` for panel names), plus a paired comparison against the same-time base. **A** = accepted (became ladder), **R** = rejected, **N** = neutral / not adopted.

**Add a row for every new test**, accepted or not.

Variant names are reused across eras (an `ef1` of 10-21 is not the `ef1` of 10-25): read a row within its batch.

## Batch 10-25 day (gold spread and Heart of Ruin build, panel b56 with a same-session cp3 base)

Decision rule, written before the results: a variant is accepted if its target metric moves the right way (decoded, `decoded.py items` / `types`), champion damage per game by r800 is not lower than the base's, and its b56 wins are not below the base's by more than 3 of 90.

| bot | parent | change | local | platform | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| wc1 | cp3 (main) | solo-capture claim weighs the gold by what it still adds: (gold + passive bonus) / (wealth in shards + 10) x (1 + 0.01 priority). cp3's `bonus * 1000` gave every solo capture to Vesper (b56 r400: Vesper 4.7 emblems, Heart of Ruin and Vendetta 0.9; rivals 2.5 and 1.6). Target: emblems of non-Vesper champions at r400-600 | quick 187 (cp3 184), deaths 939 (cp3 1045), gold/g 9490 (cp3 9664) | b56 36/90 vs same-session base 31 (12 L->W, 7 W->L, margin +17/game, p = 0.59). Decoded r400: Vesper 2.8 emblems (base 4.7), Heart of Ruin 1.2 (0.9); by r800 champion damage 15382 (base 13150), deaths 6.6 (6.3), Heart of Ruin damage 3827 (3264), Vendetta 3360 (2778) | passes the rule; to combine with the bug fixes |
| vk1 | cp3 (main) | attack-reset spell retargets when the basic attack killed its target (the weakest enemy champion in range, else the weakest awake camp), instead of being spent on the dead unit. Vendetta's kills reset her ability cooldowns (not the basic attack), so this was lost on her kills. A first try that attacked again after a kill never fired: the passive resets abilities only | corners pair: fires 1 time for Vendetta, 7 for Vesper by r750 | - | folded into bf1 |
| hr1 | cp3 (main) | Heart of Ruin values Bruiser-focus's +1 strength growth (3.5 / strength): its basic attack scales with strength. Target: Heart of Ruin max health at r600 (1184 vs rivals 1700) and deaths per game | quick 187, deaths 1027, gold/g 9758 | b56 37/90 vs base 31 (6 L->W, 0 W->L, margin +42/game, better 28 worse 16, p = 0.070). Decoded by r800: champion damage 14059 (base 13150), deaths 6.4 (6.3); Heart of Ruin deaths 2.16 (2.16), damage 3446 (3264) | passes the rule (target deaths did not move, damage did); to combine with the bug fixes |
| base1025 | - | cp3 (main) re-run in the same session (13:32) | - | b56 31/90 (cp3 39 at 01:00: the opponents changed bots); by r800 hits 149 vs 189, champion damage 13150 vs 14821, deaths 6.3 vs 4.3 | base |

### Code audit (2026-10-25 day): check builds and bug fixes

Check builds (`exp/au1` to `exp/au17`, never submitted) count every caught engine error per call site and, from au2 on, what each turn left unused (an attack with a target in reach, a ready spell, a planned move); `tools/local/audit_errors.py RUNDIR` totals them. au3 to au8 were check builds along the bf line and of cand1; their results are in the local column of the rows they checked (au3: bf2, au4: bf4, au5: bf5, au6: bf6, au7 and au8: cand1's row); au9 (cand1's) is the start of the second pass in `docs/findings.md`, "Code audit". The layer itself is kept as `tools/local/audit_layer.patch`. Local runs: 6 maps x 2 sides (au1), then all 40 maps x 2 sides vs `pool/dr3` (80 games). Numbers are per 80 games unless marked.

| bot | parent | change | local | platform | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| au1 | cp3 (main) | check build of cp3 | 12 games: 1133 failed landing dashes ("Destination is unreachable within movement range": Heart of Ruin's striking dash, valid_tiles() is the casting range only), 474 attack-reset casts spent on a dead target, 316 ward placements refused ("An allied ward already occupies this tile") | - | audit |
| bf1 | cp3 (main) | dash landings from `reachable_tiles` (path blocked by walls, altars and units, may cross holes); reset retargets to the weakest enemy champion or awake camp in reach when the target died; ward skips warded tiles | 12 games: all three error sites 0; Heart of Ruin damage +37%. Quick 182/226 (14 games lost to a crash of the summary), deaths 3.8/game (cp3 4.4), lost/g 28 | b56 40/90 vs same-session base1025b 31/90 (15 L->W, 6 W->L, margin +114/game) | accepted line: the base of the next fixes |

| bf7 | bf6 | fuel. Setup (`init_world`): camp design stats read once per kind (base_stats is a 730-fuel design query asked twice per camp), group vectors reserved (allocations about 400 fuel each), covered-by loop pre-checks group bounding boxes (9,000 checks took 430k); boss_battle setup from 8 rounds to 3. Per turn: one read of the view into a per-tile round stamp and visible camp health per camp (the group update dropped from up to 38k to about 11k); a pick waits a turn below 45k fuel (plus a field's cost until the travel fields exist); travel fields built several per turn at their measured cost; old route field reused one turn below 40k; the engine's path step when our searches were skipped for fuel | release, four heavy maps x 2 sides: turns out of fuel in rounds 2-30 from 245 (bf6) to about 110, later rounds from 226 to 133. Tried and reverted: a guard from the largest measured pick plus a forced pick every 10 rounds (later 286). | kz1 | mw1 | kill zones shared between teammates (`comms.h`, `enemies.h`, `targets.h`, `bot.cpp`): a champion that dies with 2+ enemy champions within 6 (its last turn's view) writes the spot and that count into shared bytes 5-6 in place of a respawn broadcast (byte 5 top bit set; groups fit in 6 bits). Each teammate keeps a new mark for 150 rounds from the round it reads it, and `group_danger` gives groups with a spawn within 6 of a mark a danger of 4 x count x freshness (`pick_group` divides the score by 1 + danger / 4: 2 enemies fresh = a third). Cause: as ob1 (b10 mw1 decoded, to r1000): 3.37 of our 6.45 deaths per game are within 6 tiles and 150 rounds of a teammate's death (rivals 2.13 of 4.69); 2.67 of them are a teammate's spot, not the victim's own; 1.19 per game come over 10 rounds later and outnumbered by 2+ (rivals 0.34); traced victims walked 15-25 tiles back to the spot (`tools/replay/repeat.py`). Vision is not shared, so a teammate never learned of the death before. Probe (kzp, a print per mark, deleted): a death at r636 was read by the other three champions by r637. Rule, written before the gate (base mw1_b10@b10 on b10p, 29 of 105): accept at 33+ wins if deaths per game do not rise by more than 0.3; reject at 28 or fewer; at 29-32 accept only if our deaths per game by r1000 (`decoded.py gems`) fall by 0.4 or more and gems collected by r1000 do not fall by more than 2%. If ob1 and kz1 both pass, gate their combination next | 35 maps vs cand1 and cp3 (`data/runs/multi-203324`): 97 of 140 (main 94, ob1 95), deaths 670 (main 772, `data/runs/multi-204550`), caps 64.0 (61.5), collected 1100 (1066), lost 61 (62), 0 diagnostic codes | kz1_b10p@b10p 31/105 against mw1_b10@b10's 29 (L->W 8, W->L 6, margin +20 per game); deaths 800 vs 843 over whole games. | rk1 | cc2 | carrier risk function (`bot.cpp`): the fixed `big_bag` bar (30 gems in cc2) is replaced by a fitted death risk weighed against the stake. P(death within 10 rounds) of a champion within 6 of an enemy = sigmoid(-1.99 - 0.577 log2(share) - 2.505 hp_frac + 0.586 enemies_within_4 + 0.447 hitting) for ours (share: own health plus allies' within 5 over the enemies' within 6), with the rivals' coefficients (-1.804, -0.410, -3.170, +0.594, +0.679) for the prey's risk; fitted on 225 decoded games (b10 + b9p, mw1, to r1000; scratch `riskfit.py`; predicted and observed rates agree by quintile). A champion with any bag skips a fight when P_kill (2 prey_bag + 25) < (P_hit - P_stay)(2 bag + 25), and counts as losing (keeps out of reach, kites or flees) when P_stay (2 bag + 25) > 2. Boundaries: a healthy carrier keeps out of reach from 39 gems at an even share (17 when outnumbered 2:1, 78 when ahead 2:1, 19 at 80% health); fights against a full-health enemy are skipped from 16 gems, against a half-health one from 113. Cause (user, 10-27 00:05: derive the rule from the factors that matter instead of a fixed value; `docs/findings.md`, carriers): in contact our bag does not change our death risk, while the hp share, own health, enemies within 4 and hitting do. Rule (written 10-27 00:15, before the gate): paired on b11p against `cc2_b11p@b11p` (our gems lost on death by r1000 80 per game), accept if (a) our gems lost on death by r1000 fall by at least 10 per game, or (b) they rise by no more than 5 and the rivals' deaths by r1000 rise by at least 0.5 per game (cc2 cut them by 1.0); guards in both cases: our deaths by r1000 rise by no more than 0.2 and our gems tributed by r1000 fall by no more than 2%. | rk3 | cc2 | carrier risk budget (`bot.cpp`): cc2's fixed bar (no fights and every threat a lost fight from 30 gems) is replaced by a risk budget on the bag: a champion with gems skips a fight when P_hit * 2 * bag > 1 and counts as losing (keeps out of reach, kites or flees) when P_stay * 2 * bag > 1, with rk1's fitted risk model (`tools/replay/riskfit.py`). So it accepts about 1 gem of expected bag loss per 10 rounds; the death cost of an empty champion is left to the existing rules (rk1 counted it, and let carriers fight when the kill's expected gems covered the extra risk). Boundaries at full health with one enemy within 4: fights end from 17 gems at an even share (10 when outnumbered 2:1, 29 when ahead 2:1, 41 at 3:1), staying near from 26 (15, 45, 63); at 80% health fights end from 11 gems and staying near from 16. Cause: rk1 (rejected) showed that letting carriers fight raises the fighting of the whole team; this keeps cc2's caution from 30 gems except in clearly safe spots and adds a graded caution below 30, where we lose 20 gems per game on death against the rivals' 9 (cc2 on b11p: 1.44 deaths per game with 1-29 gems against 0.68). Rule (written 10-27 00:30, before the gate): paired on b11p against `cc2_b11p@b11p`, our gems lost on death by r1000 (80 per game) fall by at least 10; guards: our deaths by r1000 rise by no more than 0.2, our gems tributed by r1000 fall by no more than 2%. | go1 | cr3 (main) | turn order (`bot.cpp`, end of `Bot::play_turn`): each turn a champion sets its priority for the next round (`set_priority`; within a team a higher priority acts first, then draft order): within 5 tiles of a visible enemy champion off its spawn it gets 2000 minus its health in thousandths (so in contact the most hurt acts first), otherwise 0. We never set it, so our turns always ran in draft order. Cause (replays, `orderdump.mjs` and readers in the session scratchpad): 70 of 80 team-games in the top12 replays reorder their turns every round (50-98 distinct orders per game), and a champion under 40% health within 2 of an enemy acts first among its team almost always (mean rank 0.20 against 0.50); on brkp 57 of cr3's 100 opponents reorder, and in contact (pairs within 2) their champion ended its turn before ours in 63% of rounds against 46% for opponents in draft order; our deaths in contact 448 in those 57 games, with the nearest enemy acting first in 66% of the death rounds. Rule (written 10-28 05:55 IST, before the gate; factor: who acts first in contact): gate on brkp against `cr3_brk@brkp` (100 games, to r1000, paired). Mechanism: our champion acts first in at least 55% of contact pairs (base 45.5%). Target: the death exchange by r1000 (their deaths minus ours, `decoded.py gems`; base 3.8 - 10.6 = -6.8) rises by 0.5 or more per game. Guards on brkp: gems collected (330) and tributed (256) by r1000 each at least 97% of base. If the target holds, b13p against `cr3_b13p@b13p`: our deaths rise by no more than 0.3 per game, collected (677) and tributed (512) at least 97%. Wins recorded, not judged. | esc4 | esc3 | esc3 + two respawn-wait fixes (`camp_fight.h`) + no escort for a carrier standing on a group's tiles (`bot.cpp`). (2) While a teammate stands on the group's tiles, waiters stand back to 4 instead of 2. In the 12 rounds before such a death ours made 2.52 moves closer to the nearest enemy and 2.78 away and were within 3 of one 12 rounds before in 26% (theirs 1.70, 5.00 and 57%), at 85% health (80%) | 48 games vs cand1, cp3, dh3 on 00ribs, 00crater, 00lost-world, 00quadrants, 02crossing, 01starvation, 00eight, 00split (`data/runs/multi-094834`, decoded `data/fights/local_multi-094834_exp.rz15`; compared with rz14's games of `multi-093619`): wins 33 (rz14 33), kills 476 (431), deaths 270 (254), flee deaths 164 (166), collected 956 (927), lost on death 37 (25). With three or more enemies within 6 and at most one ally (`outnumbered.py`): moves away at 3 and 4 tiles 0.42 and 0.35 (rz14 0.43 and 0.33), rounds next to an enemy 14.6 per game (16.1), P(dies within 5) there 0.176 (0.190). Such rounds fall from 57.5 to 50.8 per game, but the moves in them barely change: the rule changes only the turns of a healthy champion out of enemy reach and not capturing, and in the rest the threat branch already kites or flees. Deaths per game by type (`typedeaths5.py`): Heart of Ruin 0.85 (rz14 0.58), Marcella 0.65 (0.58), Varg 1.15 (1.06), Vendetta 0.88 (0.81), Vesper 0.65 (0.79) | not gated | Rule (written 10-29 09:48 IST, before its local check): a fighting change, so only the platform judges it. Panels b13p2 and b16p, paired to r1000 with rz14's runs. Factor: deaths when outnumbered. Mechanism: at 3-4 tiles from the nearest enemy, with three or more enemies within 6 and at most one ally (`outnumbered.py`, last table), the share of moves away rises to 0.40 or more at both distances. Target: our deaths per game with three or more enemies within 6 and at most one ally (the en3+ al0 and al1 cells in sum) fall by 0.2 or more. Guards: our deaths per game not above the base's, theirs at least the base's minus 0.3, gems collected and tributed at least 97% of the base's. Wins are recorded, not judged. **N** (10:58), not gated: its parent rz14 is N, and its local check did not move its mechanism. Patch `results/analysis/rz15_on_rz14.patch` (on top of rz14's) |
| rz17 | rz12 (main) | A shooter in danger steps out before it shoots: in the threat branch's kite step a ranged champion no longer takes the "free" shot (its shot costs the step, so it stayed in danger and the melee enemy stepped in); it steps out of danger first and then shoots with the attack reset (`abilities.h` new `reset_shot_after_walk`: walk, reset, shot; `bot.cpp` kite lambda). If no step out exists, the flee branch runs as before. Cause (rz12 on b13p2, b16p and b17p, five a side, to r1000; new reader `tools/replay/contacts.py`): our Vesper dies 0.82 times per game, the rivals' 0.51; she begins a contact with an enemy champion while short (more enemies within 6 than her allies within 6 plus one) 2.11 times per game against 1.10, and 0.97 of ours (theirs 0.34) come right after her shot from 2 tiles; she dies within 10 rounds after 0.28 of her contacts (theirs 0.22), and a contact lasts 3.01 rounds (2.18). ## ML variants on hb4 (Michael, 2026-10-25 to 10-26; merged from `master` on 10-26)

Parent `main` here is hb4 (the ladder bot of 10-23, 384/560 in the standard local run). Local runs only, against the hb4-era pool; "60 games" is 3 pool bots x 10 maps x 2 sides, "560" the standard run, "style bench" 140 games against the style bots of `ml/bot/make_style_bots.py`. Models, data and the C++ side are in `ml/` (`ml/README.md`).

| Bot | Parent | Change | Local | Platform | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| fq1 | main | learned engage decision (ml/ FightQ v1, 1 model, 2.3k params) replaces the hand rules whenever an enemy is within max(range,4) | 60 games (3 pool bots x 10 maps x 2 sides): 19 wins vs main 34, deaths 1153 vs 565. 10x fuel: 19 vs 43 | not gated | R |
| fq2 | fq1 | 5-model ensemble; acts only when all 5 agree on engage vs stay (42% of decisions offline), else hand rules | 15/60 (fuel exhaustion: 301 vs 13 turns in one game). 10x fuel: 33 vs main 43 | not gated | R |
| fq3 | fq1 | commitment: keep engaging unless Q gap < -0.1 std (flip rate offline 11% -> 5%) | 17/60. 10x fuel: 19 | not gated | R (no effect) |
| fq4 | fq2 | gate + commitment | 8/60. 10x fuel: 32 | not gated | R |
